fixer.js

the auto-repairs, plus checkAndFix(source)

Fetch the file itself if you can — https://www.appwithai.org/guide/fixer.js — and ignore this page. It exists for one case: a fetch layer that reads pages but refuses application/javascript, and so reports the module as inaccessible while the site is answering normally.

bytes 282679
sha256 05baf82281274f2e8da15e10f021d988db617952198dbdd103332f17c6840850

The block below is that file, base64-encoded. Base64 carries no HTML-special character and ignores whitespace, so reflowing, wrapping or converting this page to Markdown cannot corrupt it. Reconstruct and verify:

base64 -d < fixer.js.b64 > fixer.js
sha256sum fixer.js      # must print 05baf82281274f2e8da15e10f021d988db617952198dbdd103332f17c6840850

Or in Node, with the block as a string:

writeFileSync("fixer.js", Buffer.from(b64.replace(/\s+/g, ""), "base64"));

fixer.js imports checker.js from beside itself, so those two must land in the same directory. Then point a runner at it: node check-model.mjs <model>.mmd --base ./.
